# Port AERMOD to Python in one pass

You are translating the in-scope physics of EPA AERMOD 26135 (Fortran) into a
complete Python package, `aermod_py`, in a single response.

The input that follows has five parts:

1. **Contract** (`prompts/contract.md`): the entry point, what each input
   holds, the translation rules, the debug rows, and how the port is judged.
   It is binding.
2. **`harness/glue.py`**: human-written code that replaces AERMOD's SETUP
   phase and its output writers. Import it (`import glue`). Don't copy or
   re-implement it, and don't port SETUP.
3. **SETUP inputs**: every module variable your code reads but never assigns,
   with its Fortran type and rank. `run_case` receives all of them in `setup`.
4. **DEBUGOPT variables**: the only names allowed in debug rows, generated
   from real AERMOD debug output.
5. **Fortran**: a subroutine index, then the source of every program unit
   reachable from HRLOOP in this tier's scope, with `modules.f` in full.
   Calls to units whose source is not in the pack (ERRHDL, averaging, output)
   are out of scope: an ERRHDL call becomes nothing for warnings and
   informational messages, and `raise RuntimeError(...)` for fatal ('E') errors.

Work through HRLOOP in call order and translate every reachable unit. Keep the
Fortran's structure, constants and order of operations. Correctness on the
first run matters more than brevity. Watch especially for:

- 1-based to 0-based indexing (`DO I=1,N` is `range(N)`; `A(I:J)` includes J);
- integer vs real arithmetic (`NINT`, `INT`, `MOD`, integer division);
- which variable a SAVEd or MODULE value carries from one call or hour to the
  next;
- branches that depend on stability (`UNSTAB`/`STABLE`), plume state
  (`PPF`, `HSP`, penetration) or PRIME (`WAKE`) flags.

Respond with the package only: a sequence of
`=== FILE: aermod_py/<name>.py ===` blocks, starting with `__init__.py`. Put no
prose between files. Any `.py` layout under `aermod_py/` is fine; prefer one
module per Fortran file (`hrloop.py`, `metext.py`, `iblval.py`, `calc1.py`,
`calc2.py`, `prise.py`, `sigmas.py`, `prime.py`, ...) plus `state.py` for the
module-variable dataclasses.


# PART 1: Contract (prompts/contract.md)

# AERMOD Python port: contract

You are translating the in-scope physics of EPA AERMOD 26135 from Fortran to a
Python package, `aermod_py`. AERMOD's SETUP phase (runstream parsing) and its
output writers are **not** ported: `harness/glue.py`, included below, does that
work and hands your code the values SETUP would have left in the module
variables. Everything from `SUBROUTINE HRLOOP` down is yours.

Scope: POINT sources, FLAT terrain, rural, concentration only, 1-hour averages.
Tier A cases have no building data; tier B cases add PRIME downwash. Any deck
outside this scope is rejected by `glue.load_project` before your code runs.

## Entry point

`aermod_py/__init__.py` must export:

```python
def run_case(project: glue.Project, met: glue.Met, setup: dict, *,
             debug: bool = False) -> glue.Result:
```

Import the glue types with `import glue` (the harness puts `harness/` on
`sys.path`). The function must not read or write files, print, or keep state
between calls.

`glue.Result` has three fields:

- `dates`: int64 array, the KURDAT (YYMMDDHH, 2-digit year) of every hour HRLOOP
  processes, in order. It must equal `glue.expected_dates(project, met)`, which
  is every surface record inside the STARTEND window (ISDATE..IEDATE).
- `conc`: float64 array of shape `(len(dates), NUMREC)`. Row h, column IREC-1
  is the 1-hour average AERMOD writes to the POSTFILE for hour h and receptor
  IREC, in ug/m^3, for the POSTFILE source group: the sum over the group's
  sources (`IGROUP(ISRC, IGRP) == 1`) of HRVAL. Do not round; the harness
  writes the POSTFILE.
- `debug`: list of tuples, empty unless `debug=True` (see "Debug rows").

## Inputs

**`setup: dict`** holds every module variable the in-scope Fortran reads but
never assigns. Its keys are exactly the names in `harness/setup_inputs.json`
(the Fortran names, upper case). Values follow the Fortran declaration:

| Fortran | Python |
|---|---|
| DOUBLE PRECISION scalar | `float` |
| INTEGER scalar | `int` |
| LOGICAL scalar | `bool` |
| CHARACTER scalar | `str` (trailing blanks stripped) |
| array | `numpy.ndarray` with the Fortran shape: `A(I,J)` is `a[I-1, J-1]` |
| CHARACTER array | object array of `str` |
| unallocated ALLOCATABLE | `None` (never indexed by in-scope code) |

These values are checked bit for bit against an instrumented AERMOD that dumps
the module variables just before `CALL HRLOOP`. Values set by a DATA statement
or PARAMETER (for example `GRIDHT`) and every variable the in-scope code
assigns itself are not in `setup`; take them from the Fortran.

**`project: glue.Project`** is the parsed deck. You need little of it beyond
`setup`; it is there for `ISDATE`/`IEDATE`, the receptor count, and to pass to
`glue.expected_dates`.

**`met: glue.Met`** replaces METEXT's file reads:

- `met.surface[NAME][h]` is the value the list-directed
  `READ(MFUNIT, *) IYEAR, IMONTH, ..., NCLOUD` assigns for surface record h
  (0-based, counting every data record in the file, including those outside
  STARTEND). Names are the READ list in `glue.SURFACE_READ`.
- `met.profile[NAME][j]` is the value of `READ(IPROFN, *) KYEAR, ..., PFLSW`
  for profile row j. Hour h's levels are rows
  `met.profile_start[h]:met.profile_start[h+1]`, in file order.
- `met.sfc_headers` holds the header line(s) (lines containing a colon).

Translate each READ of a data record into taking the next record from these
arrays, and each BACKSPACE into stepping back. METEXT's `READ(...,'(A256)')
BUFFER` reads only feed messages and header checks; you may drop them along
with any statement whose only effect is an ERRHDL message. ERRHDL itself is not
ported: a warning or informational call becomes nothing, a fatal ('E') call
becomes `raise RuntimeError(...)`. Keep every assignment that a later
calculation reads. The look-ahead READ under `HBPLUME`
is out of scope (`HBPLUME` is `False` in `setup`).

HRLOOP's own date handling decides which records are processed: records before
ISDATE are read and skipped, processing stops after IEDATE or at the end of the
file.

## Translation rules

1. Every Fortran DOUBLE PRECISION value is a Python `float` or numpy
   `float64`. Nothing in scope is single precision.
2. Keep constants exactly as written, including `D0` literals, and keep the
   order of operations where floating-point results could differ: `A*B/C` is
   not `A*(B/C)`.
3. Keep branch order and loop structure. Nested IFs stay nested; GOTOs become
   loops with `break`/`continue` or flags. Don't merge or simplify branches.
4. Fortran intrinsics map to `math` (scalars) or numpy (arrays): `DSQRT` is
   `sqrt`, `DLOG` is `log`, `DLOG10` is `log10`, `NINT` rounds half away from
   zero (not Python's `round`), `INT` truncates toward zero, `MOD` takes the sign of
   the first argument (`math.fmod` for reals; not Python's `%`), `SIGN(A,B)` is `math.copysign(abs(A), B)`.
   Integer division truncates toward zero.
5. Comment every 1-based to 0-based index change, for example
   `# Fortran ISRC=1..NUMSRC -> isrc in range(NUMSRC)`.
6. MODULE variables and SAVEd locals become fields of explicit dataclasses
   passed between functions. No module-level mutable state.
7. Every function's docstring names the Fortran unit it comes from, for example
   `"""SUBROUTINE PCALC (calc1.f)."""`.
8. The hour loop is explicit. Within an hour you may vectorise over receptors
   with `import numpy as xp` (so the port can later run on JAX), provided the
   arithmetic per receptor is the same as the Fortran's.

## Debug rows

With `debug=True`, emit the values AERMOD prints under
`CO DEBUGOPT MODEL METEOR PRIME`, as tuples

    (KURDAT, ISRC, IREC, comp, sub, var, value)

in the order AERMOD computes them. The allowed `(sub, comp, var)` triples are
listed in `prompts/debug_vars.md`, which is generated from real AERTEST
debug files written by our compiled AERMOD. Use only those names. For
example: `METDEB` `USTAR`, `WSTAR`, `ZI`, `OBULEN`, `UEFF`; `PCALC` `DHCRIT`,
`XFINAL`, `DHFAER`; `IBLVAL`/`PAN` `UEFF`; `DELTAH`/`PLM` `DHP`; `AER_PCHI`/`PAN`
`AEROUT`; `PCALC`/`PRIME` `HRVAL`.

- `KURDAT`: the hour, YYMMDDHH with a 2-digit year (as in `Result.dates`).
- `ISRC`: 1-based source index; 0 for METDEB rows.
- `IREC`: 1-based receptor index for rows printed per receptor, 0 for rows
  printed once per hour or once per source-hour. For METDEB `GRID*` rows it is
  the 1-based grid level.
- `comp`: `"PAN"` inside the meander/pancake pass, `"PLM"` inside the coherent
  plume pass, `"PRIME"` for PRIME, `""` otherwise. The `level` column of
  `debug_vars.md` says which kind of key each name takes.
- `sub`: the Fortran subroutine whose WRITE prints the value.
- `var`: the name in `debug_vars.md`. Names with a dot (`GAUSSIAN.SY`) are a
  column of a DEBOUT table row; names with an operator are the printed
  expression.
- If AERMOD prints the same `(KURDAT, ISRC, IREC, comp, sub, var)` more than once
  (iterations in DELTAH, NUMRISE, ...), emit one row per print in the same order.

`harness/debugdiff.py` compares these rows with the parsed reference: the first
value that differs by more than one unit in AERMOD's last printed digit, or the
first row you didn't emit, is what the repair loop works on.

## How the port is judged

`harness/run_port.py` checks `Result` (dates, shape, dtype, finite values) and
writes the POSTFILE. `harness/compare.py` then scores it on every case:

- **EPA rule**: against EPA's reference POSTFILE (our binary's, for the two
  cases EPA ships none), each of the three "best-fit slope" figures EPA's kit
  uses (through-origin least squares, the R script's `lm` line, and the ratio
  of mean concentrations) must be within 1 ± 0.001.
- **Strict rule** against our compiled binary: every (hour, receptor) row
  present; zero/nonzero agreement ≥ 99.9%; p99 relative error ≤ 1e-4 on values
  ≥ 1% of the case maximum; every receptor's H4H (4th-highest daily maximum)
  design value within 1e-4 relative.

One case is held out and never shown to the repair loop.

## Output format

Emit each file of the package with a marker line, and nothing else outside
file contents:

```
=== FILE: aermod_py/__init__.py ===
<contents>
=== FILE: aermod_py/hrloop.py ===
<contents>
```

Only `.py` files under `aermod_py/` are accepted. Markdown fences around
contents are stripped.


# PART 2: harness/glue.py (import it; do not port it)

```python
"""Human-written glue between a case deck and the generated port.

AERMOD's SETUP phase and its output writers are not ported. This module does
their job for the scoped slice and hands the port arrays named after the MAIN1
module variables SETUP would have filled:

  * ``load_project(inp)`` -> ``Project``: the deck's sources (after PPARM's
    floors and PROFBASE's ZBASE reassignment), receptor networks expanded in
    AERMOD's order (INCLUDED files too), EMISFACT, STARTEND -> ISDATE/IEDATE.
    pyaermod parses the deck as a cross-check; options this module does not
    model are rejected.
  * ``load_met(project)`` -> ``Met``: the values METEXT's list-directed READ
    statements would assign, one array per READ-list variable.
  * ``setup_values(project, met)`` -> dict: every module variable the port reads
    but never assigns (harness/setup_inputs.json), as SETUP leaves it before
    HRLOOP. Numbers go through ``stodbl``, a port of AERMOD's runstream parser.
  * ``expected_dates`` and ``Result``: what the port's run_case must return.
  * ``write_postfile(...)``: a 1-hr PLOT POSTFILE in the FORMAT AERMOD uses.

Coordinates use the same double-precision expressions as the Fortran (reset.f
GENCAR/SETCAR/GENPOL/SETPOL/DISCAR/DISPOL/EVCART). tests/test_harness.py checks
receptors and the POSTFILE round trip against every oracle POSTFILE, and
setup_values against the SETUP.DMP that ref/aermod_dump writes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import math
import re
import warnings
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import numpy as np
from pyaermod.input_generator import AERMODProject
from pyaermod.input_reader import read_aermod_input
from pyaermod.sources import PointSource

PI = 4.0 * math.atan(1.0)             # setup.f VARINI
DTORAD = PI / 180.0
IEDATE_NONE = 2147123124              # setup.f: no STARTEND
BUILDING_KEYS = {"BUILDHGT": "ADSBH", "BUILDWID": "ADSBW", "BUILDLEN": "ADSBL",
                 "XBADJ": "ADSXADJ", "YBADJ": "ADSYADJ"}
EMISFACT_SIZES = {"SEASON": 4, "MONTH": 12, "HROFDY": 24, "WSPEED": 6, "SEASHR": 96,
                  "HRDOW": 72, "HRDOW7": 168, "SHRDOW": 288, "SHRDOW7": 672,
                  "MHRDOW": 864, "MHRDOW7": 2016}
SURFACE_READ = ["IYEAR", "IMONTH", "IDAY", "IJDAY", "IHOUR", "SFCHF", "USTAR", "WSTAR",
                "VPTGZI", "ZICONV", "ZIMECH", "OBULEN", "SFCZ0", "BOWEN", "ALBEDO", "UREF",
                "WDREF", "UREFHT", "TA", "TREFHT", "IPCODE", "PRATE", "RH", "SFCP", "NCLOUD"]
SURFACE_INT = {"IYEAR", "IMONTH", "IDAY", "IJDAY", "IHOUR", "IPCODE", "NCLOUD"}
PROFILE_READ = ["KYEAR", "KMONTH", "KDAY", "KHOUR", "PFLHT", "JFLAG", "PFLWD", "PFLWS",
                "PFLTA", "PFLSA", "PFLSW"]
PROFILE_INT = {"KYEAR", "KMONTH", "KDAY", "KHOUR", "JFLAG"}
PATHWAYS = {"CO", "SO", "RE", "ME", "OU", "EV"}
RE_KEYWORDS = {"GRIDCART", "GRIDPOLR", "DISCCART", "DISCPOLR", "EVALCART", "ELEVUNIT",
               "INCLUDED", "STARTING", "FINISHED"}
GRID_SUBKEYS = {"STA", "END", "XYINC", "XPNTS", "YPNTS", "ORIG", "DIST", "DDIR", "GDIR",
                "ELEV", "HILL", "FLAG"}
# Keywords and MODELOPT options this module models and some case exercises (so
# setup_values is checked against AERMOD for them). Anything else is rejected so
# an unmodelled option never passes silently; add a case before widening these.
KNOWN_KEYWORDS = {
    "CO": {"STARTING", "FINISHED", "TITLEONE", "TITLETWO", "MODELOPT", "AVERTIME", "POLLUTID",
           "RUNORNOT", "ERRORFIL"},
    "SO": {"STARTING", "FINISHED", "ELEVUNIT", "LOCATION", "SRCPARAM", "BUILDHGT", "BUILDWID",
           "BUILDLEN", "XBADJ", "YBADJ", "EMISFACT", "SRCGROUP"},
    "ME": {"STARTING", "FINISHED", "SURFFILE", "PROFFILE", "SURFDATA", "UAIRDATA", "SITEDATA",
           "PROFBASE", "STARTEND"},
    "OU": {"STARTING", "FINISHED", "RECTABLE", "POSTFILE", "FILEFORM"},
}
MODELOPT_ALLOWED = {"CONC", "FLAT", "ELEV", "BETA", "ALPHA", "WARNCHKD"}
ME_IMSTAT = {"STARTING": 1, "SURFFILE": 2, "PROFFILE": 3, "SURFDATA": 4, "UAIRDATA": 5,
             "STARTEND": 6, "SITEDATA": 9, "PROFBASE": 10, "FINISHED": 50}   # meset.f MECARD
DAY_OF_WEEK_QFLAGS = {"HRDOW", "HRDOW7", "SHRDOW", "SHRDOW7", "MHRDOW", "MHRDOW7"}
# modules.f DATA GRIDHT, first 29 levels: soset.f locates stack tops in these (<= 600 m).
GRIDHT_29 = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0, 14.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0, 60.0, 70.0, 80.0,
             90.0, 100.0, 120.0, 140.0, 160.0, 180.0, 200.0, 250.0, 300.0, 350.0, 400.0,
             450.0, 500.0, 550.0, 600.0)
# Module variables harness/setup_inputs.py flags but that the ported code only ever
# assigns through a CALL argument or names as an IOSTAT= specifier.
SETUP_SCRATCH = {"FNUM", "IMIT", "IOERRN", "IOSTAT"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Source:
    """One POINT source, fields named after MAIN1 source arrays (deck order = ISRC)."""
    isrc: int                          # 1-based AERMOD source index
    SRCID: str
    AXS: float
    AYS: float
    AZS: float
    AQS: float                         # g/s
    AHS: float
    ATS: float
    AVS: float
    ADS: float
    flat: bool                         # LOCATION ... FLAT (L_FlatSrc)
    building: Optional[dict[str, np.ndarray]] = None   # ADSBH/ADSBW/ADSBL/ADSXADJ/ADSYADJ (36)
    QFLAG: Optional[str] = None        # EMISFACT type, e.g. "HRDOW"
    QFACT: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    in_group: bool = True


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Receptors:
    """Receptors in AERMOD's index order (IREC = position + 1)."""
    AXR: np.ndarray
    AYR: np.ndarray
    AZELEV: np.ndarray
    AZHILL: np.ndarray
    AZFLAG: np.ndarray
    NETID: tuple[str, ...]
    RECTYP: tuple[str, ...]

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.AXR)


@dataclass
class Project:
    inp: Path
    deck: AERMODProject                # pyaermod's parse, for anything not lifted out below
    MODELOPT: tuple[str, ...]          # CO MODELOPT keywords, upper case
    POLLUT: str
    FLAT: bool
    ELEV: bool
    FLGPOL: bool
    AZFLAG_DEFAULT: float
    ISDATE: int
    IEDATE: int
    ZBASE: float                       # ME PROFBASE
    sources: list[Source]              # every source in the deck, ISRC order
    group: str                         # POSTFILE source group
    receptors: Receptors
    file_format: str                   # "FIX" or "EXP" (OU FILEFORM)
    cards: dict[str, list[list[str]]] = field(default_factory=dict)   # pathway -> token lists
    title: str = ""

    @property
    def group_sources(self) -> list[Source]:
        return [s for s in self.sources if s.in_group]


@dataclass
class Met:
    sfc_path: Path
    pfl_path: Path
    surface: dict[str, np.ndarray]     # one entry per SURFACE_READ name, one row per record
    profile: dict[str, np.ndarray]     # one entry per PROFILE_READ name, one row per level
    profile_start: np.ndarray          # hour h uses profile rows profile_start[h]:profile_start[h+1]
    sfc_headers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.surface["IYEAR"])


@dataclass
class Result:
    """What the port's run_case returns (prompts/contract.md)."""
    dates: np.ndarray                  # int64 KURDAT (YYMMDDHH), one per hour HRLOOP processed
    conc: np.ndarray                   # float64 [hour, receptor], ug/m^3, POSTFILE group total
    debug: list[tuple] = field(default_factory=list)
    # debug rows: (KURDAT, ISRC, IREC, comp, sub, var, value) in the order AERMOD would
    # print them; names and keys as in prompts/debug_vars.md (harness/dbgparse.py).


# --- deck tokenising ---------------------------------------------------------

def _cards(path: Path, pathway: str) -> list[list[str]]:
    """Token lists of every card in ``pathway`` (INCLUDED files expanded), pathway dropped."""
    out: list[list[str]] = []
    current = None
    for raw in path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines():
        if raw.lstrip().startswith("**") or not raw.strip():
            continue
        toks = raw.split()
        if toks[0].upper() in PATHWAYS:
            current = toks[0].upper()
            toks = toks[1:]
        if current != pathway or not toks:
            continue
        if toks[0].upper() == "INCLUDED" and len(toks) > 1:
            inc = path.parent / toks[1]
            out += _cards_included(inc, pathway)
            continue
        out.append(toks)
    return out


def _cards_included(path: Path, pathway: str) -> list[list[str]]:
    out = []
    for raw in path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines():
        if raw.lstrip().startswith("**") or not raw.strip():
            continue
        toks = raw.split()
        if toks[0].upper() in PATHWAYS:
            if toks[0].upper() != pathway:
                continue
            toks = toks[1:]
        if toks:
            out.append(toks)
    return out


def stodbl(text: str) -> tuple[float, int]:
    """AERMOD's STODBL: (value, repeat count) of one field, bit-for-bit.

    AERMOD reads every runstream number this way, digit by digit in double
    precision, which is not correctly rounded (``62.26`` -> 62.260000000000005),
    so ``float()`` would give the port different inputs from AERMOD's.
    """
    cnum, head, fdec, imuti = 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1
    nmark = pmark = dmark = mmark = emark = False
    started = False
    for chk in text.upper():
        if chk == " ":
            if started:
                break
            continue
        started = True
        if "0" <= chk <= "9":
            if not dmark:
                cnum = cnum * 10.0 + float(ord(chk) - 48)
            else:
                fdec = fdec / 10.0
                cnum = cnum + fdec * float(ord(chk) - 48)
        elif dmark and not emark and chk in "ED":
            emark = True
            head = -cnum if nmark else cnum
            dmark = nmark = False
            cnum = 0.0
        elif not pmark and chk == "+":
            pmark = True
        elif not nmark and chk == "-":
            nmark = True
        elif not dmark and chk == ".":
            dmark = True
        elif not mmark and chk == "*" and not nmark:
            mmark = True
            imuti = _nint(cnum)
            cnum = 0.0
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid number {text!r}")
    fnum = -cnum if nmark else cnum
    if emark:
        fnum = head * 10.0 ** fnum if abs(fnum) <= 30.0 else (
            0.0 if fnum < 0.0 else head * 10.0 ** 30.0 if fnum > 0.0 else fnum)
    return fnum, imuti


def _nint(x: float) -> int:
    """Fortran NINT/IDNINT: round half away from zero."""
    return int(math.copysign(math.floor(abs(x) + 0.5), x))


def num(text: str) -> float:
    value, count = stodbl(text)
    if count != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"repeat count not allowed here: {text!r}")
    return value


def _numbers(tokens: list[str]) -> list[float]:
    """STODBL every field, expanding AERMOD 'n*value' repeats."""
    out: list[float] = []
    for t in tokens:
        value, count = stodbl(t)
        out += [value] * count
    return out


# --- receptors ---------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class _Grid:
    kind: str
    netid: str
    xint: float = 0.0
    yint: float = 0.0
    xs: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)      # XCOORD (distances for polar)
    ys: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)      # YCOORD (directions for polar)
    rows: dict[str, dict[int, list[float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _expand_grid(g: _Grid, src_xy: dict[str, tuple[float, float]], acc: dict) -> None:
    """SETCAR / SETPOL: J (y or direction) outer, I (x or distance) inner."""
    for j, yc in enumerate(g.ys, start=1):
        for i, xc in enumerate(g.xs, start=1):
            if g.kind == "GRIDPOLR":
                ytemp = yc * DTORAD
                acc["AXR"].append(g.xint + xc * math.sin(ytemp))
                acc["AYR"].append(g.yint + xc * math.cos(ytemp))
            else:
                acc["AXR"].append(xc)
                acc["AYR"].append(yc)
            for key, arr in (("ELEV", "AZELEV"), ("HILL", "AZHILL"), ("FLAG", "AZFLAG")):
                row = g.rows.get(key, {}).get(j)
                acc[arr].append(row[i - 1] if row else None)
            acc["NETID"].append(g.netid)
            acc["RECTYP"].append("GP" if g.kind == "GRIDPOLR" else "GC")


def expand_receptors(cards: list[list[str]], src_xy: dict[str, tuple[float, float]],
                     elev: bool, flgpol: bool, azflag_default: float) -> Receptors:
    acc: dict[str, list] = {k: [] for k in ("AXR", "AYR", "AZELEV", "AZHILL", "AZFLAG",
                                            "NETID", "RECTYP")}
    grid: Optional[_Grid] = None
    last_kw = None
    for toks in cards:
        kw = toks[0].upper()
        if kw in RE_KEYWORDS:
            last_kw, args = kw, toks[1:]
        else:
            kw, args = last_kw, toks
        if kw in (None, "STARTING", "FINISHED", "ELEVUNIT"):
            continue
        if kw in ("GRIDCART", "GRIDPOLR"):
            if args and args[0].upper() not in GRID_SUBKEYS:
                netid, args = args[0].upper(), args[1:]
            else:
                netid = grid.netid if grid else ""
            sub = args[0].upper()
            vals = args[1:]
            if sub == "STA":
                grid = _Grid(kw, netid)
            elif grid is None:
                raise ValueError(f"{kw} {sub} before STA")
            elif sub == "END":
                _expand_grid(grid, src_xy, acc)
                grid = None
            elif sub == "XYINC":
                x0, nx, dx, y0, ny, dy = (num(v) for v in vals[:6])
                grid.xs = [x0 + dx * float(i) for i in range(_nint(nx))]
                grid.ys = [y0 + dy * float(j) for j in range(_nint(ny))]
            elif sub == "XPNTS":
                grid.xs += _numbers(vals)
            elif sub == "YPNTS":
                grid.ys += _numbers(vals)
            elif sub == "ORIG":
                if re.fullmatch(r"[-+.\dEeDd]+", vals[0]):
                    grid.xint, grid.yint = num(vals[0]), num(vals[1])
                else:
                    grid.xint, grid.yint = src_xy[vals[0].upper()]
            elif sub == "DIST":
                grid.xs += _numbers(vals)
            elif sub == "DDIR":
                grid.ys += _numbers(vals)
            elif sub == "GDIR":
                n, dirini, dirinc = _nint(num(vals[0])), num(vals[1]), num(vals[2])
                for j in range(n):
                    d = dirini + dirinc * float(j)
                    if d > 360.0:
                        d -= 360.0
                    elif d <= 0.0:
                        d += 360.0
                    grid.ys.append(d)
            elif sub in ("ELEV", "HILL", "FLAG"):
                grid.rows.setdefault(sub, {}).setdefault(_nint(num(vals[0])), []).extend(
                    _numbers(vals[1:]))
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"unsupported {kw} subkey {sub}")
            continue
        if kw == "DISCCART":
            v = [num(t) for t in args]
            ze = zh = zf = None
            if elev and flgpol:
                ze = v[2] if len(v) >= 3 else None
                zh = v[3] if len(v) >= 4 else None
                zf = v[4] if len(v) == 5 else None
            elif elev:
                ze = v[2] if len(v) >= 3 else None
                zh = v[3] if len(v) >= 4 else None
            elif flgpol:
                zf = v[2] if len(v) == 3 else (v[4] if len(v) == 5 else None)
            _append(acc, v[0], v[1], ze, zh, zf, "", "DC")
            continue
        if kw == "DISCPOLR":
            xs, ys = src_xy[args[0].upper()]
            v = [num(t) for t in args[1:]]
            rng, direct = v[0], v[1]
            ze = zh = zf = None
            if elev:
                ze = v[2] if len(v) >= 3 else None
                zh = v[3] if len(v) >= 4 else None
                zf = v[4] if flgpol and len(v) == 5 else None
            elif flgpol:
                zf = v[2] if len(v) == 3 else (v[4] if len(v) == 5 else None)
            _append(acc, xs + rng * math.sin(direct * DTORAD), ys + rng * math.cos(direct * DTORAD),
                    ze, zh, zf, "", "DP")
            continue
        if kw == "EVALCART":
            v = [num(t) for t in args[:5]]
            _append(acc, v[0], v[1], v[2] if elev else None, v[3] if elev else None,
                    v[4] if flgpol else None, "", "DC")
            continue
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported RE keyword {kw}")

    def arr(name: str, default: float) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([default if v is None else v for v in acc[name]], dtype=np.float64)

    return Receptors(
        AXR=np.array(acc["AXR"], dtype=np.float64), AYR=np.array(acc["AYR"], dtype=np.float64),
        AZELEV=arr("AZELEV", 0.0), AZHILL=arr("AZHILL", 0.0), AZFLAG=arr("AZFLAG", azflag_default),
        NETID=tuple(acc["NETID"]), RECTYP=tuple(acc["RECTYP"]))


def _append(acc: dict, x, y, ze, zh, zf, netid: str, rectyp: str) -> None:
    for k, v in (("AXR", x), ("AYR", y), ("AZELEV", ze), ("AZHILL", zh), ("AZFLAG", zf),
                 ("NETID", netid), ("RECTYP", rectyp)):
        acc[k].append(v)


# --- sources -----------------------------------------------------------------

def _emisfact(cards: list[list[str]]) -> dict[str, tuple[str, list[float]]]:
    out: dict[str, tuple[str, list[float]]] = {}
    for toks in cards:
        if toks[0].upper() != "EMISFACT":
            continue
        sid, qflag = toks[1].upper(), toks[2].upper()
        if "-" in sid:
            raise ValueError(f"EMISFACT source range {sid} not supported")
        prev = out.get(sid, (qflag, []))
        if prev[0] != qflag:
            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: EMISFACT {qflag} after {prev[0]}")
        out[sid] = (qflag, prev[1] + _numbers(toks[3:]))
    for sid, (qflag, vals) in out.items():
        if len(vals) != EMISFACT_SIZES[qflag]:
            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: EMISFACT {qflag} has {len(vals)} values, "
                             f"expected {EMISFACT_SIZES[qflag]}")
    return out


def _group_members(cards: list[list[str]], group: str) -> Optional[set[str]]:
    if group.upper() == "ALL":
        return None
    members = {t.upper() for toks in cards
               if toks[0].upper() == "SRCGROUP" and toks[1].upper() == group.upper()
               for t in toks[2:]}
    return members


def _pparm(ats: float, avs: float, ads: float) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """soset.f PPARM's adjustments to a POINT source's exit parameters."""
    if ats == 0.0:
        ats = -1.0e-5
    elif 0.0 < ats < 200.0:
        raise ValueError(f"stack temperature {ats} K < 200 K (AERMOD error 320)")
    if avs < 1.0e-5:
        avs = 1.0e-5
    if ads < 0.0:
        raise ValueError("negative stack diameter (AERMOD error 209)")
    if ads < 1.0e-5:
        ads = 1.0e-5
    return ats, avs, ads


def _sources(so_cards: list[list[str]], group: str, flatsrcs: bool) -> list[Source]:
    """soset.f SOLOCA/SOPARM/PPARM/DSFILL/EFFILL for POINT sources, deck order = ISRC."""
    emis = _emisfact(so_cards)
    members = _group_members(so_cards, group)
    loc: dict[str, tuple[float, float, float, bool]] = {}
    parm: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    bld: dict[str, dict[str, list[float]]] = {}
    for toks in so_cards:
        kw = toks[0].upper()
        if kw == "LOCATION":
            sid, styp = toks[1].upper(), toks[2].upper()
            if styp != "POINT":
                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: only POINT sources are in scope, got {styp}")
            flat = len(toks) == 6 and toks[5].upper() == "FLAT"
            if flat and not flatsrcs:
                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: LOCATION ... FLAT needs MODELOPT FLAT ELEV")
            z = num(toks[5]) if len(toks) == 6 and not flat else 0.0
            loc[sid] = (num(toks[3]), num(toks[4]), z, flat)
        elif kw == "SRCPARAM":
            parm[toks[1].upper()] = [num(t) for t in toks[2:7]]
        elif kw in BUILDING_KEYS:
            if "-" in toks[1]:
                raise ValueError(f"{kw} source range {toks[1]} not supported")
            bld.setdefault(toks[1].upper(), {}).setdefault(BUILDING_KEYS[kw], []).extend(
                _numbers(toks[2:]))
    out = []
    for i, (sid, (x, y, z, flat)) in enumerate(loc.items(), start=1):
        building = None
        if sid in bld:
            if set(bld[sid]) != set(BUILDING_KEYS.values()) or any(
                    len(v) != 36 for v in bld[sid].values()):
                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: needs all five building keywords with 36 sectors each")
            building = {k: np.array(v) for k, v in bld[sid].items()}
        aqs, ahs, ats, avs, ads = parm[sid]
        ats, avs, ads = _pparm(ats, avs, ads)
        qflag, qfact = emis.get(sid, (None, None))
        out.append(Source(
            isrc=i, SRCID=sid, AXS=x, AYS=y, AZS=z, AQS=aqs, AHS=ahs, ATS=ats, AVS=avs, ADS=ads,
            flat=flat, building=building, QFLAG=qflag,
            QFACT=None if qfact is None else np.asarray(qfact, dtype=np.float64),
            in_group=members is None or sid in members))
    return out


def _check_against_pyaermod(deck: AERMODProject, sources: list[Source]) -> None:
    """pyaermod parses the same deck independently; disagreement means a glue bug."""
    theirs = deck.sources.sources
    if [s.source_id.upper() for s in theirs] != [s.SRCID for s in sources]:
        raise AssertionError("glue and pyaermod disagree on the source list")
    for p, s in zip(theirs, sources):
        if not isinstance(p, PointSource):
            raise AssertionError(f"pyaermod does not see {s.SRCID} as a POINT source")
        pairs = [(p.x_coord, s.AXS), (p.y_coord, s.AYS), (p.emission_rate, s.AQS),
                 (p.stack_height, s.AHS)]
        if p.building_height is not None or s.building is not None:
            pairs += list(zip(p.building_height, s.building["ADSBH"]))
        for a, b in pairs:
            if not math.isclose(float(a), b, rel_tol=1e-12, abs_tol=1e-12):
                raise AssertionError(f"{s.SRCID}: pyaermod {a} vs glue {b}")


# --- control / dates ---------------------------------------------------------

def _cent(yy: int) -> int:
    """meset.f CENT_DATE for a 2-digit year."""
    if yy > 99:
        return yy
    return (1900 if yy >= 50 else 2000) + yy


def _startend(me_cards: list[list[str]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    for toks in me_cards:
        if toks[0].upper() != "STARTEND":
            continue
        v = [int(t) for t in toks[1:]]
        if len(v) == 6:
            isyr, ismn, isdy, ieyr, iemn, iedy = v
            ishr, iehr = 1, 24
        elif len(v) == 8:
            isyr, ismn, isdy, ishr, ieyr, iemn, iedy, iehr = v
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"STARTEND with {len(v)} fields")
        isyr, ieyr = _cent(isyr), _cent(ieyr)
        return (isyr * 1000000 + ismn * 10000 + isdy * 100 + ishr,
                ieyr * 1000000 + iemn * 10000 + iedy * 100 + iehr)
    return 0, IEDATE_NONE


def _postfile_group(ou_cards: list[list[str]]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    group, fmt = None, "FIX"
    for toks in ou_cards:
        kw = toks[0].upper()
        if kw == "POSTFILE":
            if toks[1] != "1" or toks[3].upper() != "PLOT":
                raise ValueError("only a 1-hr PLOT POSTFILE is supported")
            group = toks[2].upper()
        elif kw == "FILEFORM":
            fmt = toks[1].upper()
    if group is None:
        raise ValueError("deck has no POSTFILE")
    return group, fmt


def load_project(inp: Path | str) -> Project:
    inp = Path(inp)
    reader_log = logging.getLogger("pyaermod.input_reader")
    level = reader_log.level
    reader_log.setLevel(logging.ERROR)      # it warns about every keyword this module parses itself
    try:
        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
            deck = read_aermod_input(inp)
    finally:
        reader_log.setLevel(level)
    co, so, re_, me, ou = (_cards(inp, p) for p in ("CO", "SO", "RE", "ME", "OU"))
    modelopt = tuple(t.upper() for toks in co if toks[0].upper() == "MODELOPT" for t in toks[1:])
    for pathway, cards in (("CO", co), ("SO", so), ("ME", me), ("OU", ou)):
        unknown = {t[0].upper() for t in cards} - KNOWN_KEYWORDS[pathway]
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"out of scope {pathway} keywords: {sorted(unknown)}")
    if set(modelopt) - MODELOPT_ALLOWED:
        raise ValueError(f"out of scope MODELOPT: {sorted(set(modelopt) - MODELOPT_ALLOWED)}")
    for toks in so + re_:
        if toks[0].upper() == "ELEVUNIT" and toks[1].upper() != "METERS":
            raise ValueError(f"ELEVUNIT {toks[1]} not supported (METERS only)")
    if "FLAT" not in modelopt:
        raise ValueError("MODELOPT FLAT is required (flat terrain is the port's scope)")
    elev = "ELEV" in modelopt                  # coset.f: FLAT plus ELEV sets FLATSRCS
    flgpol, azflag_default = False, 0.0        # FLAGPOLE is rejected above
    group, fmt = _postfile_group(ou)
    sources = _sources(so, group, flatsrcs=elev)
    _check_against_pyaermod(deck, sources)
    src_xy = {s.SRCID: (s.AXS, s.AYS) for s in sources}
    isdate, iedate = _startend(me)
    zbase = next((num(t[1]) for t in me if t[0].upper() == "PROFBASE"), 0.0)
    # meset.f PROFBASE: under FLAT, sources (only FLAT ones when FLATSRCS) sit at ZBASE.
    sources = [replace(s, AZS=zbase) if (s.flat or not elev) else s for s in sources]
    receptors = expand_receptors(re_, src_xy, elev, flgpol, azflag_default)
    if not elev:
        n = len(receptors)
        receptors = replace(receptors, AZELEV=np.full(n, zbase), AZHILL=np.full(n, zbase))
    return Project(
        inp=inp, deck=deck, MODELOPT=modelopt,
        POLLUT=next((t[1].upper() for t in co if t[0].upper() == "POLLUTID"), ""),
        FLAT="FLAT" in modelopt, ELEV=elev, FLGPOL=flgpol, AZFLAG_DEFAULT=azflag_default,
        ISDATE=isdate, IEDATE=iedate, ZBASE=zbase, sources=sources, group=group,
        receptors=receptors,
        file_format=fmt, cards={"CO": co, "SO": so, "RE": re_, "ME": me, "OU": ou},
        title=deck.control.title_one or "")


# --- meteorology -------------------------------------------------------------

def _read_free(path: Path, names: list[str], ints: set[str],
               headers: Optional[list[str]] = None) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    cols: dict[str, list] = {n: [] for n in names}
    for line in path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        if ":" in line:
            if headers is not None:
                headers.append(line.rstrip())
            continue
        toks = line.split()
        if len(toks) < len(names):
            raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: short record {line!r}")
        for n, t in zip(names, toks):
            cols[n].append(int(t) if n in ints else float(t))
    return {n: np.array(v, dtype=np.int64 if n in ints else np.float64) for n, v in cols.items()}


def load_met(project: Project, met_dir: Path | str | None = None) -> Met:
    """Read the deck's SURFFILE/PROFFILE (looked up in ``met_dir``, default the deck's dir)."""
    base = Path(met_dir) if met_dir else project.inp.parent
    sfc = base / Path(project.deck.meteorology.surface_file).name
    pfl = base / Path(project.deck.meteorology.profile_file).name
    headers: list[str] = []
    surface = _read_free(sfc, SURFACE_READ, SURFACE_INT, headers)
    profile = _read_free(pfl, PROFILE_READ, PROFILE_INT)
    return Met(sfc_path=sfc, pfl_path=pfl, surface=surface, profile=profile,
               profile_start=_profile_start(surface, profile), sfc_headers=headers)


def expected_dates(project: Project, met: Met) -> np.ndarray:
    """KURDAT of every surface record inside STARTEND (what HRLOOP writes to the POSTFILE)."""
    s = met.surface
    year = np.where(s["IYEAR"] < 100, [_cent(int(y)) for y in s["IYEAR"]], s["IYEAR"])
    mdh = s["IMONTH"] * 10000 + s["IDAY"] * 100 + s["IHOUR"]
    full = year * 1000000 + mdh
    keep = (full >= project.ISDATE) & (full <= project.IEDATE)
    return ((year % 100) * 1000000 + mdh)[keep].astype(np.int64)


def _profile_start(surface: dict[str, np.ndarray], profile: dict[str, np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Row offsets of each hour's profile levels; the profile file must track the surface file."""
    pkey = np.stack([profile[k] for k in ("KYEAR", "KMONTH", "KDAY", "KHOUR")], axis=1)
    skey = np.stack([surface[k] for k in ("IYEAR", "IMONTH", "IDAY", "IHOUR")], axis=1)
    starts = np.flatnonzero(np.r_[True, np.any(pkey[1:] != pkey[:-1], axis=1)])
    if len(starts) != len(skey) or not np.array_equal(pkey[starts], skey):
        raise ValueError(f"profile file has {len(starts)} hours that do not line up with "
                         f"the {len(skey)} surface records")
    return np.r_[starts, len(pkey)].astype(np.int64)


# --- MAIN1 values SETUP assigns ----------------------------------------------

def _locate(parray: tuple[float, ...], value: float) -> int:
    """LOCATE(PARRAY, 1, len, VALUE, NDXBLW): 1-based index of the level at or below VALUE."""
    jl, ju = 0, len(parray) + 1
    while ju - jl > 1:
        jm = (ju + jl) // 2
        if value >= parray[jm - 1]:
            jl = jm
        else:
            ju = jm
    return jl


def _julian(year: int, month: int, day: int) -> int:
    return (date(year, month, day) - date(year, 1, 1)).days + 1


def _station(me: list[list[str]], keyword: str) -> tuple[int, int, str]:
    """meset.f SURFID/UAIRID: station id, year, name ('UNKNOWN' when omitted)."""
    toks = next(t for t in me if t[0].upper() == keyword)
    return (_nint(float(toks[1])), _nint(float(toks[2])),
            toks[3].upper() if len(toks) > 3 else "UNKNOWN")


def _met_file(me: list[list[str]], keyword: str) -> str:
    toks = next(t for t in me if t[0].upper() == keyword)
    if len(toks) > 2 and toks[2].upper() != "FREE":
        raise ValueError(f"{keyword} format {toks[2]!r} not supported (FREE only)")
    return toks[1]


def setup_values(project: Project, met: Met) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Every module variable the ported code reads that only SETUP assigns.

    Keys are the Fortran names listed in harness/setup_inputs.json (minus
    SETUP_SCRATCH); values have the Fortran shape (column-major indexing, so
    ADSBH[k-1, isrc-1] is ADSBH(K,ISRC)); None means AERMOD leaves the array
    unallocated, so the ported code must not touch it. tests/test_glue.py checks
    every value against a SETUP.DMP written by ref/aermod_dump.
    """
    srcs, rec = project.sources, project.receptors
    nsrc, co, me = len(srcs), project.cards["CO"], project.cards["ME"]
    zeros = np.zeros(nsrc)
    ave = next(t[1:] for t in co if t[0].upper() == "AVERTIME")
    kave = [int(t) for t in ave if t.isdigit()]
    nqf = max((EMISFACT_SIZES[s.QFLAG] for s in srcs if s.QFLAG), default=0)
    qfact = None
    if nqf:
        qfact = np.zeros((nqf, nsrc))
        for s in srcs:
            if s.QFACT is not None:
                qfact[:len(s.QFACT), s.isrc - 1] = s.QFACT
    nsec = 36 if any(s.building is not None for s in srcs) else 0
    bld = {}
    for key in ("ADSBH", "ADSBW", "ADSBL", "ADSXADJ", "ADSYADJ"):
        bld[key] = None
        if nsec:
            bld[key] = np.zeros((nsec, nsrc))
            for s in srcs:
                if s.building is not None:
                    bld[key][:, s.isrc - 1] = s.building[key]
    imstat = np.zeros(50, dtype=np.int64)
    for t in me:
        imstat[ME_IMSTAT[t[0].upper()] - 1] += 1
    idsurf, _, sfname = _station(me, "SURFDATA")
    iduair, iuyear, uaname = _station(me, "UAIRDATA")
    header = met.sfc_headers[0] if met.sfc_headers else ""
    isjday = 0
    if project.ISDATE:
        y, rest = divmod(project.ISDATE, 1000000)
        isjday = _julian(y, rest // 10000, rest // 100 % 100)
    modelopt = set(project.MODELOPT)

    values: dict[str, object] = {
        # sources (soset.f PPARM / SOCARD, deck order)
        "NSRC": nsrc, "NUMSRC": nsrc,
        "SRCID": np.array([s.SRCID for s in srcs], dtype=object),
        "SRCTYP": np.array(["POINT"] * nsrc, dtype=object),
        "AXS": np.array([s.AXS for s in srcs]), "AYS": np.array([s.AYS for s in srcs]),
        "AZS": np.array([s.AZS for s in srcs]), "AQS": np.array([s.AQS for s in srcs]),
        "AHS": np.array([s.AHS for s in srcs]), "ATS": np.array([s.ATS for s in srcs]),
        "AVS": np.array([s.AVS for s in srcs]), "ADS": np.array([s.ADS for s in srcs]),
        "ADSFACT": np.ones(nsrc),
        "L_FLATSRC": np.array([s.flat for s in srcs]),
        "NDXSTK": np.array([_locate(GRIDHT_29, s.AHS) for s in srcs], dtype=np.int64),
        "QFLAG": np.array([s.QFLAG or "" for s in srcs], dtype=object), "QFACT": qfact,
        "L_DAYOFWEEKOPTS": any(s.QFLAG in DAY_OF_WEEK_QFLAGS for s in srcs),
        "NSEC": nsec, **bld,
        "IGROUP": np.array([[1 if s.in_group else 0] for s in srcs], dtype=np.int64),
        "NUMGRP": 1,
        "AFTSRC": np.array(["N"] * nsrc, dtype=object),
        "URBSRC": np.array(["N"] * nsrc, dtype=object),
        "EVAL": np.zeros(nsrc, dtype=bool), "L_HRLYSIG": np.zeros(nsrc, dtype=bool),
        "OSPLAT": np.zeros(nsrc, dtype=bool),
        "INPD": np.zeros(nsrc, dtype=np.int64), "HOUREMISARRAY": np.zeros(nsrc, dtype=np.int64),
        **{k: zeros.copy() for k in ("AAFR", "ABA", "ABH", "ABL", "ABW", "ABYPR", "AFP",
                                     "AMFUEL", "ARPWR", "ASRCANGLE", "ASYINI", "ASZINI",
                                     "ATHRUST", "AVAA", "HENRY", "PLATHB", "PLATWB")},
        # receptors (reset.f)
        "NUMREC": len(rec), "AXR": rec.AXR, "AYR": rec.AYR, "AZELEV": rec.AZELEV,
        "AZHILL": rec.AZHILL, "AZFLAG": rec.AZFLAG,
        # control (coset.f)
        "POLLUT": project.POLLUT, "ELEV": project.ELEV, "L_WARNCHKD": "WARNCHKD" in modelopt,
        "NUMAVE": len(kave), "KAVE": np.array(kave, dtype=np.int64),
        "PERIOD": any(t.upper() == "PERIOD" for t in ave),
        "ANNUAL": any(t.upper() == "ANNUAL" for t in ave),
        **{k: np.zeros(len(kave), dtype=np.int64) for k in ("IDYTAB", "ITOXFL", "ITXUNT")},
        # meteorology (meset.f, and the surface file header meset.f reads)
        "METINP": _met_file(me, "SURFFILE"), "PROINP": _met_file(me, "PROFFILE"),
        "METFRM": "FREE", "PROFRM": "FREE",
        "IDSURF": idsurf, "IDUAIR": iduair, "IUYEAR": iuyear, "SFNAME": sfname,
        "UANAME": uaname, "ZBASE": project.ZBASE, "IEDATE": project.IEDATE,
        "ISJDAY": isjday, "IMSTAT": imstat,
        "L_ADJUSTAR": "ADJ_U*" in header, "L_BULKRN": "BULKRN" in header,
        # setup.f VARINI constants
        "PI": PI, "TWOPI": 2.0 * PI, "RTOFPI": math.sqrt(PI), "SRT2PI": math.sqrt(2.0 * PI),
        "RTOF2": math.sqrt(2.0), "RTPIBY2": math.sqrt(PI / 2.0), "RT2BYPI": math.sqrt(2.0 / PI),
        "DTORAD": DTORAD,
    }
    values.update(SCOPE_FIXED)
    missing = set(SETUP_INPUT_NAMES) - SETUP_SCRATCH - set(values)
    if missing:
        raise AssertionError(f"setup_values does not provide {sorted(missing)}")
    return values


# Values SETUP assigns that are the same for every deck load_project accepts: the
# defaults of options outside the scope, unit numbers, and fixed tuning constants.
# None = left unallocated (array for an out-of-scope source type or option).
SCOPE_FIXED: dict[str, object] = {
    **{k: None for k in ("AALPHA", "AANGLE", "APDEFF", "APDENS", "APDIAM", "APHI", "ATSTOP",
                         "AVGRAV", "AXCNTR", "AXINIT", "AXVERT", "AYCNTR", "AYINIT", "AYVERT",
                         "IURBGRP", "NVERTS", "URBPOP", "URBZ0", "VDEP", "WASHOUT")},
    **{k: False for k in (
        "ARM2", "AWMADWDBG", "DAYTAB", "DDEP", "DEBUG", "DEPOS", "DFAULT", "EVONLY",
        "FASTAREA", "GRSM", "HOURLY", "LDGAS", "LDPART", "LUSERVD", "LWGAS", "LWPART",
        "L_AWMA_ENTRAIN", "L_AWMA_UEFF", "L_AWMA_UTURB", "L_AWMA_UTURBHX", "L_BACKGRND",
        "L_BGHOURLY", "L_BGSECTOR", "L_BLSOURCE", "L_EFFSIGY", "L_MAXDCONT", "L_NOXHOURLY",
        "L_NOXSECTOR", "L_O3HOURLY", "L_O3SECTOR", "L_ORD_CAV", "L_ORD_TURB", "L_ORD_UEFF",
        "L_PBAL", "L_RECT_BLDG", "L_SKIPMESSAGES", "L_STRMLN_BLDG", "L_USERFRANMAX",
        "L_USERFRANMIN", "L_VECTORWS", "METEORDBG", "MONTH", "MULTYR", "MXFILE", "NO2AVE",
        "NOBID", "NOCHKD", "NOSTD", "O3MISS", "OLM", "PLATFMDBG", "PM25AVE", "PRIMEDBG",
        "PSDCREDIT", "PVMRM", "RSTINP", "RSTSAV", "RUNTTRM", "RUNTTRM2", "SCIM", "SCIMOUT",
        "SCREEN", "SEASONHR", "SO2AVE", "SUMMFILE", "TXFILE", "URBAN", "URBDBUG", "WDEP")},
    **{k: True for k in ("CLMPRO", "CONC", "L_URBANTRANSITION", "MSGPRO", "PPFILE")},
    **{k: np.zeros(6, dtype=bool) for k in ("L_BGVALUES", "L_NOXVALUE", "L_NOX_VALS",
                                            "L_O3VAL", "L_O3VALUES")},
    "TURBOPTS": np.zeros(9, dtype=bool),
    "BGSECT": np.zeros(6), "NOXSECT": np.zeros(6), "O3SECT": np.zeros(6),
    "NOXBACK": np.full(6, -999.0), "O3BACK": np.full(6, -999.0),
    "RL_GRIDWS": np.zeros((87, 3)), "EMIFAC": np.array([1.0e6]),    # CONC, g/s -> ug/m^3
    "IPROCL": np.ones(366, dtype=np.int64), "ISEAHR": np.zeros(1, dtype=np.int64),
    **{k: 0 for k in ("FULLHRQ", "IEVENT", "IPAIR", "IYR", "I_ALPHA", "NDX4ZI", "NREGINT",
                      "NREGSTART", "NUMBLGRPS", "NUMURB", "NUMYRS", "NURB")},
    **{k: 1 for k in ("INCRST", "NUMBGSECTS", "NUMNOXSECTS", "NUMO3SECTS", "NUMTYP")},
    "NYEARS": 5,
    "AWMADWDBUNT": 34, "DBGUNT": 24, "DBMUNT": 25, "IOUNIT": 8, "ISUMUNT": 28, "MFUNIT": 19,
    "MPUNIT": 21, "PLATFMDBUNT": 36, "PRMDBUNT": 27, "URBUNT": 37, "URBUNT1": 38,
    "URBUNT2": 40,
    **{k: 0.0 for k in ("DENOM", "EPSEFF", "EPSEFF3", "EPSEFFD", "FRACSAT", "FRANMIN",
                        "PLUME_SPREAD", "ROTANG", "SVAVG", "SWAVG", "UAVG", "USERVD", "ZRDEP")},
    "BIGT": 24.0, "EXPLIM": -50.0, "FRANMAX": 1.0, "MAXDIST": 1.0e20, "SVMIN": 0.2,
    "SWMIN": 0.02, "SZCOEF": 2.15, "WSMIN": 0.2828,
}
SETUP_INPUT_NAMES = tuple(v["name"] for v in json.loads(
    (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "setup_inputs.json").read_text()))


# --- output ------------------------------------------------------------------

POSTFILE_COLUMNS = ("*        X             Y      AVERAGE CONC    ZELEV    ZHILL    ZFLAG"
                    "    AVE     GRP       DATE     NET ID")
POSTFILE_RULE = ("* ____________  ____________  ____________   ______   ______   ______  ______"
                 "  ________  ________  ________")


def _e13_6(v: float) -> str:
    """Fortran E13.6 (0.dddddd E+xx), as gfortran prints it."""
    if v == 0.0:
        return f"{'0.000000E+00':>13s}"
    m, e = f"{v:.5E}".split("E")
    digits = m.replace("-", "").replace(".", "")
    exp = int(e) + 1
    mant = f"0.{digits}"
    if len(mant) < 8:
        mant += "0" * (8 - len(mant))
    s = f"{'-' if v < 0 else ''}{mant}E{'+' if exp >= 0 else '-'}{abs(exp):02d}"
    return f"{s:>13s}"


def format_row(x: float, y: float, conc: float, ze: float, zh: float, zf: float,
               group: str, date: int, netid: str, file_format: str) -> str:
    """One record in AERMOD's PSTFRM (setup.f / coset.f)."""
    c = _e13_6(conc) if file_format == "EXP" else f"{conc:13.5f}"
    return (f" {x:13.5f} {y:13.5f} {c} {ze:8.2f} {zh:8.2f} {zf:8.2f}  {'1-HR':>6s}"
            f"  {group:<8s}  {date:08d}  {netid:<8s}")


def write_postfile(path: Path | str, project: Project, dates: np.ndarray, conc: np.ndarray) -> None:
    """conc[hour, receptor] in ug/m^3 for the POSTFILE group; dates are YYMMDDHH ints."""
    r = project.receptors
    if conc.shape != (len(dates), len(r)):
        raise ValueError(f"conc shape {conc.shape} != ({len(dates)}, {len(r)})")
    fmt = ("(2(1X,F13.5),1(1X,E13.6),3(1X,F8.2),2X,A6,2X,A8,2X,I8.8,2X,A8)"
           if project.file_format == "EXP" else
           "(3(1X,F13.5),3(1X,F8.2),2X,A6,2X,A8,2X,I8.8,2X,A8)")
    lines = [
        f"* AERMOD (26135 ): {project.title}",
        "* AERMET ( 26135):",
        f"* MODELING OPTIONS USED:   {'  '.join(project.MODELOPT)}",
        f"*         POST/PLOT FILE OF CONCURRENT  1-HR VALUES FOR SOURCE GROUP: {project.group:<8s}",
        f"*         FOR A TOTAL OF {len(r):5d} RECEPTORS.",
        f"*         FORMAT: {fmt}",
        POSTFILE_COLUMNS,
        POSTFILE_RULE,
    ]
    for h, kurdat in enumerate(dates):
        for i in range(len(r)):
            lines.append(format_row(r.AXR[i], r.AYR[i], float(conc[h, i]), r.AZELEV[i],
                                    r.AZHILL[i], r.AZFLAG[i], project.group, int(kurdat),
                                    r.NETID[i], project.file_format))
    Path(path).write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
```

# PART 4: DEBUGOPT variables (prompts/debug_vars.md)

# DEBUGOPT variables (generated)

Generated by `harness/dbgcatalog.py` from `CO DEBUGOPT MODEL METEOR [PRIME]` runs of
our compiled AERMOD 26135 on aertest_nobldg, CAPPED_STACK2CE, AERTEST, CAPPED_STACK1C0, ALLSRCS_STACKDW. Do not edit by hand.

Each row is one name `harness/dbgparse.py` reads from the Fortran WRITE statement
in subroutine `sub`. `comp` is `PAN` (meander/pancake pass), `PLM` (coherent plume
pass), `PRIME`, or empty. Names with a dot (`DIRECT1.HED1`) are a column of a
DEBOUT plume table row; names with an operator (`CLIFT*EMIFAC`) are the printed
expression.

## METEOR.DBG

| sub | comp | var | level | cases |
|---|---|---|---|---|

##### FORTRAN FILE modules.f
##### UNIT MAIN1 (MODULE) modules.f:1-1821
      MODULE MAIN1
C***********************************************************************
C     MAIN1
C     AERMOD Model Data - Parameter, Variable and Array Declarations
C                         Global Data for All Modules
C
C***********************************************************************

      IMPLICIT NONE

c ----------------------------------------------------------------------
c --- AERMOD
c
c     Modified to accept additional building data required by
c     the PRIME building downwash modules
c
c     Changes are denoted in comment fields
c
c     Prepared by    Earth Tech, Inc
c     Prepared for   EPRI under contract WO3527-01
c ----------------------------------------------------------------------

C***********************************************************************
C     User Specified Model Parameters for Array Dimensions
C***********************************************************************

C --- Most array limits for data storage are now allocated at runtime.
      INTEGER, PARAMETER :: NWSCAT= 6, NKST= 6, NHR= 24,
     &                      NPAIR= 100, NHIANN= 10,
     &                      NMXPM= 10, MXPLVL=50, MXGLVL=87

      INTEGER :: NYEARS

C**   NWSCAT = Number of Wind Speed Categories
C**   NKST   = Number of Stability Categories
C**   NHR    = Number of Hours in Met Data Loop
C**   NPAIR  = Number of Pairs of TXCONC and IDCONC for TOXXFILE Output
C**   NHIANN = Number of high period/annual averages to include in the
C**                   summary page of the output file (formerly controlled
C**                   by NVAL)
C**   NMXPM  = Number of high average High-N-High 24-hour PM2.5 averages or
C**                   High-N-High 1-hour NO2 or SO2 averages to include in the
C**                   summary table for PM-2.5/NO2/SO2 processing across years
C**   MXPLVL = Maximum number of levels in the observed profile file
C**   MXGLVL = Maximum number of levels in the gridded profiles (0 - 4000 m)
C**   NYEARS = Number of Years allowed for multi-year analyses for PM2.5,
C**                   1-hour NO2 and 1-hour SO2 standards, which are averaged
C**                   over the number of years modeled; a default value of 5
C**                   years is assumed, but the user can override the default
C**                   using the ME NUMYEARS keyword; e.g., setting NYEARS = 1
C**                   on the ME NUMYEARS keyword for 1 year of site-specific
C**                   met data will significantly reduce the memory requirements
C**                   for the MAXDCONT option.

C** The following array limits are set dynamically at runtime:
C**   NSRC   = Max Number of Sources
C**   NREC   = Max Number of Receptors
C**   NGRP   = Max Number of Source Groups
C**   NAVE   = Max Number of Short Term Averaging Periods
C**   NVAL   = Max Number of High Values by Receptor (RECTABLE Keyword)
C**   NTYP   = Max Number of Output Types per Run (CONC, DEPOS, DDEP and WDEP)
C**   NMAX   = Max Number of Overall Maximum Values (MAXTABLE Keyword)
C**   NSEC   = Number of Sectors for Building Dimensions
C**   NQF    = Number of Variable Emission Rate Factors Per Source
C**   NBF    = Number of Temporally Varying Background Concentrations
C**   NO3F   = Number of Temporally Varying Ozone Concentrations
C**   NNOXF   = Number of Temporally Varying NOx Concentrations
C**   NPDMAX = Max Number of Particle Diameter Categories Per Source
C**   NVMAX  = Max Number of Vertices for AREA/AREACIRC/AREAPOLY and/or
C**            OPENPIT Sources
C**   IXM    = Max Number of X-coord (Distance) Values Per Receptor Network
C**   IYM    = Max Number of Y-coord (Direction) Values Per Receptor Network
C**   NNET   = Max Number of Cartesian and/or Polar Receptor Networks
C**   NARC   = Maximum number of Receptor Groupings ('ARCs') for EVALCART
C**   NURB   = Maximum number of Urban Areas


C***********************************************************************
C     Programmer Specified Model Parameters
C***********************************************************************

CRLM increase IERRN from 509 to 515 for added VBARRIER E/W messages WSP 20250806
CRLM increase IERRN from 506 to 509 for added BAREDGE E/W messages WSP 20240911
CRLM increase IERRN from 500 to 506 for added SBARRIER E/W messages WSP 20240619
CRLM  IKN increased from 120 to 121 to add SBARRIER WSP 20240507
CRLM  IKN increased from 121 to 122 to add VBARRIER WSP 20250225
CJAT  06/10/2020:  ISSUE D53, ADDED FROM 19191; ADD 1 TO IERRN TO
C     REFLECT NEW ERROR CODE 126
CCRT  02/16/2021:  ISSUE D062 - MINSW, BIGT, ADD 2 TO IERRN TO
C     REFLECT NEW ERROR CODES AND AVOID CONFLICTS WITH PAST CODE MERGES
C      INTEGER, PARAMETER :: IFMAX=150, IKN=102, ISTRG=512, ILEN_FLD=200,
C     &                      IERRN=317
CCRCO 2/18/2021 - need to check IKN and IERRN after merge
C      INTEGER, PARAMETER :: IFMAX=150, IKN=117, ISTRG=512, ILEN_FLD=200,
C     &                      IERRN=500
      INTEGER, PARAMETER :: IFMAX=150, IKN=122, ISTRG=512, ILEN_FLD=200,
     &                      IERRN=515
C**   IFMAX  = Max Number of Fields Per Runstream Record
C**   IKN    = Number of Keywords
C**   ISTRG  = Max Length of Runstream Image Record
C**   ILEN_FLD = Max Length of Runstream Input Fields.  Also used
C**              to specify length of input filenames and formats.
C**   IERRN  = Array length of Error/Warning/Informational Message arrays          


C***********************************************************************
C     Model Constants Specified as Parameters
C***********************************************************************

      DOUBLE PRECISION, PARAMETER ::
     &                    G = 9.80616D0,       VONKAR = 0.4D0,
     &                    GOVRCP = 0.00977D0,  DCTODK = 273.16D0,
     &                    BETA1  = 0.6D0,      BETA2  = 0.4D0,
     &                    AT1PT2 = 1.2D0,      UMINGR = 0.01D0,
     &                    GSIGV  = 0.073864D0, EFOLDH = 0.44D0,
C**   CRT 9/11/2020, D062 User Minimum Sigma W    
C**     &                    SVUMIN = 0.05D0,     SWMIN  = 0.02D0,
     &                    SVUMIN = 0.05D0,
     &                    XVAL   = 0.0D0,      SPTGMN = 0.002D0,
     &                    BSUBC  = 0.5D0,      ALPHAR = 1.4D0,
     &                    LAMDAY = 2.3D0,      ASUBE  = 0.1D0,
     &                    REFPOP = 2.0D+6,     DELTRUR= 12.0D0,
     &                    RGAS   = 8.3145D0,   
     &                    AWMA_BETA_ENTRAINCOEFF = 0.35D0,
C**  Added for Aircraft Plume Rise; UNC-IE
C     &                    R00 = 2.0D0, ALPHAM = 0.10D0,          ! Removed R00 on January 13th 2026 as it is computed within ARISE.f
     &                    ALPHAM = 0.10D0,                              
     &                    PAA = 1.013D+5, RAA = 287.058D0,
     &                    HFUEL = 4.3D+7, CPA = 1003.0D0,
     &                    NENG = 2.0D0              ! Added NENG on January 13th 2026
C    Description of the variables/constants used in ARISE
C    R00    = Aircraft Engine Exhaust Radius (m)(as Initial Plume Radius)
C    ALPHAM = Entrainment Constant (Momentum Part of ARISE)
C    PAA    = Ambient Pressure (N/m2)
C    RAA    = Gas Constant of Air (J/kg-K)
C    HFUEL  = Heating Value of Fuel (J/kg)
C    CPA    = Specific Heat of Air (J/kg-K)
C    NENG   = Number of Engines                                   ! Added on January 13th 2026
C**  End Aircraft Plume Rise insert; April 2023

C --- AWMA version D20350
C     AWMA_Beta_EntrainCoeff  = entrainment coefficient for PRIME downwash; 
C                  changes valuefrom m 0.60 for bet0 and betap when 
C                  ALPHA option AWMAEntrain is specified  (July 2020)

      DOUBLE PRECISION :: SZCOEF    ! SZCOEF

C**   Declare SVMIN variable for minimum sigma-v, formerly specified as
C**   PARAMETER SVMIN.  Default value of 0.2D0 is initialized in SUBROUTINE
C**   MODOPT; (SVMIN was increased to 0.5D0 under the old LOWWIND1 BETA option,
C**   and increased to 0.3D0 under the old LOWWIND2 and LOWWIND3 options)
C**   optional user inputs under the CO LOW_WIND keyword.
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: SVMIN

C**   Declare WSMIN variable for minimum wind speed, formerly set to 0.2828
C**   based on SQRT(2*SVmin*SVmin), where SVmin was 0.2. The default value
C**   of WSMIN is also set to 0.2828, but may be adjusted under the LOWWIND
C**   BETA options using the CO LOW_WIND keyword.
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: WSMIN

C**   Declare FRANMAX variable for maximum meander factor, FRAN, used in
C**   the LowWind2 BETA option.  The "default" value for the LowWind2
C**   option is set at 0.95, but can be modified by the user under the
C**   optional LOW_WIND keyword on the CO pathway, within a range of
C**   0.50 to 1.0, inclusive.
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: FRANMAX
      
C**   3/18/2022 D127 - add FRANMIN for meander testing -Wood
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: FRANMIN
      
C**   CRT 9/11/2020, D062 User Minimum Sigma W
C**   Declare SWMIN variable for user-defined minimum sigma-w formerly
C**   specified as PARAMETER SWMIN.  The default value will be set to 
C**   the former parameter value = 0.02 m/s, but can be modified under
C**   the optional LOW_WIND keyword on the CO pathway within a range of 
C**   0.00 to 3.00 which is consistent with the default range check by
C**   AERMET when SWnn is included as an onsite variable.
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: SWMIN

C**   RCO 9/27/2020, D061 User BIGT values
C**   Declare BIGT variable for user-defined BIGT value, formerly
C**   specified as PARAMETER BIGT.  The default value will be set to 
C**   the former parameter value = 12 hours, but can be modified under
C**   the optional LOW_WIND keyword on the CO pathway within a range of 
C**   0.50 to 48.00. Previous LOWWIND2 option set at 12 hours, not 
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: BIGT


C**   Set concentration unit conversion factors for NO2, SO2, CO, and 03
C**   for use with OZONEVAL, OZONEFIL, O3VALUES, and BACKGRND keywords;
C**   factors defined by pollutant for PPB-to-UG/M3 and PPM-to-UG/M3,
C**   based on reference temperature (25 C) and pressure (1013.25 mb).
C**   Note that factors for NO2 and SO2 are PPB/(UG/M3) and PPM/(UG/M3),
C**   and factors for CO and O3 are for (UG/M3)/PPB and (UG/M3)/PPM.
      DOUBLE PRECISION, PARAMETER ::
     &                    NO2_PPB = 0.5319D0, NO2_PPM = 0.5319D-3,
     &                    SO2_PPB = 0.3823D0, SO2_PPM = 0.3823D-3,
     &                    CO_PPB  = 1.144D0,  CO_PPM  = 1.144D3,
     &                    O3_PPB  = 1.960D0,  O3_PPM  = 1.960D3


      DOUBLE PRECISION :: PI, TWOPI, RTOFPI, SRT2PI, RTOF2,
     &                    RTPIBY2, RT2BYPI, 
     &                    DTORAD, RTODEG,
     &                    THIRD, TWOTHIRDS

C**   PI     = PI               ! Initialized in sub.VARINI as 4.0D0*DATAN(1.0D0)
C**   TWOPI  = 2.*PI            ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   RTOFPI = SQRT(PI)         ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   SRT2PI = SQRT(2.*PI)      ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   RTOF2  = SQRT(2.)         ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   RTPIBY2= SQRT(PI/2.)      ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   RT2BYPI= SQRT(2./PI)      ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   DTORAD = Degrees to Radians Conversion   ! Initialized in sub.VARINI as PI/180.0D0
C**   RTODEG = Radians to Degrees Conversion   ! Initialized in sub.VARINI as 180.0D0/PI
C**   THIRD  = 1.0/3.0          ! Initialized in sub.VARINI
C**   TWOTHIRDS  = 2.0/3.0      ! Initialized in sub.VARINI

C**   G      = Acceleration Due to Gravity (m/s**2)
C**   VONKAR = von Karman constant
C**   GOVRCP = Gravity divided by specific heat at constant pressure
C**   DCTODK = Degrees Celsius to kelvin conversion factor
C**   BETA1  = Coeff. in the calculation of 'direct' plume rise
C**   BETA2  = Coeff. in the calculation of buoyancy-induced dispersion
C**   AT1PT2 = The fraction of the mixed layer and above the mixed
C              layer through which a variable changes its value
C**   UMINGR = Minimum value for a gridded wind speed
C**   GSIGV  = Constant used in converting sigma_A to sigma_V
C**   EFOLDH = Constant in computation of dTHETA/dZ in stable atmosphere
C**   SVUMIN = Minimum value applied to Sigma_V / U when calculating
C**            Sigma_Y.
C**   SVMIN  = Minimum value applied to measured Sigma_V values
C**   SWMIN  = Minimum value applied to measured Sigma_W values
C**   BIGT   = Random scaling time parameter 
C**   XVAL   = Gradient in the miXing layer (unstable atmosphere)
C**   SPTGMN = Minimum vert. potential temp. gradient for stable atmosphere
C**   BSUBC  = Constant used in computing sigma_Z for surface layer releases
C**   SZCOEF = Coefficient of sigma-z to define the plume half-width
C**   ALPHAR = Parameter used in January 18, 1995 indirect source MCA
C**   LAMDAY = Parameter used in January 18, 1995 indirect source MCA
C**   ASUBE  = Parameter used in January 18, 1995 indirect source MCA
C**   REFPOP = Reference population for urban option (4/1/96 MCA)
C**   DELTRUR= Surface cooling in the rural area (4/1/96 MCA)
C**   RGAS   = ideal gas law constant = 8.3145 Pa-m^3/mol-K
C**

C**   NUMSYEFF = Number of effective sigma-y's to use with the
C**              FASTALL non-DFAULT option;
C**              receptors more than NUMSYEFF*SYEFF off the plume
C**              centerline are skipped to optimize runtime
      DOUBLE PRECISION, PARAMETER :: NUMSYEFF  = 4.0D0

C**   MAXDIST = Maximum transport distance for calculation;
C**             set to 80km for FASTALL or FASTAREA options;
C**             this was formerly associated with the TOXICS
C**             option, which is now obsolete.
C**             Set to 1.0D20 for applications w/o FASTALL or FASTAREA
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: MAXDIST

C**   FULLHRQ  Date/Time Stamp for Hourly Emissions Data

C***********************************************************************
C     This is The Global Variable Definition Block for the New Area
C     Source Algorithm - 7/7/93
C
C*    Added XVERT,YVERT - Jayant Hardikar, PES, 7/20/94
C***********************************************************************

C**   NVMAX and NVMAX2 are now dynamically allocated at runtime for
C**   AREAPOLY and AREACIRC sources.  If AREACIRC sources are included,
C**   then the maximum number of vertices needed for AREACIRC sources
C**   is based on the maximum number specified by the user using the
C**   optional NVERTS parameter + 4.  The default number of vertices
C**   for an AREACIRC source is still set at 20 vertices, but that
C**   value is only used to determine array storage if the the input
C**   file includes an AREACIRC source without the NVERTS parameter.
C**   If AREAPOLY sources are included, then NVMAX is set to the maximum
C**   number vertices specified for an AREAPOLY source + 8 (but not
C**   less than the number needed for AREACIRC sources).  The '+ 8'
C**   allows for the additional number of sides on an AREAPOLY source that
C**   may be needed to define the portion of the source being integrated,
C**   depending on the source/receptor/wind direction geometry.  This
C**   allows for some complexity in the shape of an AREAPOLY source, but
C**   very complex shapes may result in runtime errors (E406) and should
C**   be avoided.
C**   If no AREACIRC or AREAPOLY sources are included, but rectangular
C**   AREA sources and/or OPENPIT sources are included, then the value
C**   of NVMAX is set to 8 (4 sides plus 4).

C**   NVMAX2= NVMAX * 2

C**   Assign NVPOLY parameter for the number of additional vertices to
C**   include for AREAPOLY sources, beyond the number of vertices used
C**   to define the source.  This is needed to account for the integrated
C**   portion of the area intersecting several sides of the polygon,
C**   depending on the geometry of the source, receptor, and wind direcion.
C**   The NVPOLY parameter is assigned a value of 12, which should work
C**   in most cases.  NVPOLY is added to NVMAX in subroutine SRCSIZ in
C**   the 'aermod.f' source file.
      INTEGER, PARAMETER :: NVPOLY = 12

      INTEGER :: IVERT, NVERT, NSEGS,
     &           NVMAX, NVMAX2, NPIT, NPNT, NVOL, NLINE, NBLINE,
     &           NAREA, NPOLY, NVTEMP, NCIRC, NSWP,
     &           NPTEMP
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: UVERT(:), VVERT(:), VNVERT(:),
     &                                 WVERT(:), UASEGS(:), UBSEGS(:),
     &                                 XVERT(:), YVERT(:)
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: SPA(:,:)
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: AXINIT(:), AYINIT(:), AANGLE(:),
     &                                 AXVERT(:,:), AYVERT(:,:),
     &                                 RADIUS(:), AXCNTR(:), AYCNTR(:)
      INTEGER, ALLOCATABLE :: NVERTS(:)

      LOGICAL LSEG


C***********************************************************************
C     This is The Global Variable Definition Block for the New OPENPIT
C     Source Algorithm - 7/19/94
C***********************************************************************


      DOUBLE PRECISION, PARAMETER   :: ALPHA = 0.029D0
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: AALPHA(:), APDEFF(:), AVOLUM(:)
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: EFRAC(:), QPART(:)
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: PALPHA, THETA, PDEFF, PDREL, PITFRA, QEFF
      DOUBLE PRECISION :: PITLEN, PITWID, PITL, EMIHGT, XEFF, YEFF


C*    ALPHA     = Proportionality Constant for determining Escape Fraction
C*    AALPHA    = Array of Angles of Long Pit Dimension w.r.t.
C*                North for Each Source
C*    PALPHA    = Angle of Long Pit Dimension w.r.t. North for
C*                the Current Source
C*    THETA     = Wind Direction angle w.r.t Long Axis
C*                of the Pit
C*    APDEFF    = Array of Effective Pit Depths for Each Source
C*    PDEFF     = Effective Pit Depths for Current Source
C*    PDREL     = Relative Pit Depth
C*    AVOLUM    = Array of Volume of the OPENPIT Sources
C*    EFRAC     = Array of Escape Fractions
C*    QPART     = Array of Adjusted Emission Rates
C*    PITFRA    = Fractional Size of the Effective Pit Area
C*    PITLEN    = Length of the Pit
C*    PITWID    = Width of the Pit
C*    PITL      = Along-Wind Length of the Pit
C*    EMIHGT    = Height of Emissions Above Base of Pit
C*    XEFF      = X-dimension of Effective Pit
C*    YEFF      = Y-dimension of Effective Pit



c----------------------------------------------------------------------
c --- COMMON BLOCK /DRYGAS/ -- Dry deposition parameters        CALPUFF
c----------------------------------------------------------------------
c
      DOUBLE PRECISION, ALLOCATABLE :: pdiff(:),pdiffw(:),rmolwt(:),
     &                                 alphas(:),react(:),henry(:),
     &                                 rcli(:),finemass(:), scf(:)

      LOGICAL, ALLOCATABLE :: L_METHOD2(:)

      DOUBLE PRECISION :: Wold, Wnew, f2, EsTa

C
C     REFSPE      - Reference Species (Default is SO2)
c
c --- COMMON BLOCK /DRYGAS/ Variables:
c       PDIFF(NSRC) - real    - Molecular diffusivity (m**2/s)
c                               of each pollutant.
c                               SEE NOTE #1
c      PDIFFW(NSRC) - real    - Molecular diffusivity in water (m**2/s)
c                               of each pollutant.
c                               SEE NOTE #1
c      RMOLWT(NSRC) - real    - Molecular weight of pollutant (g/mol)
c                               of each pollutant.
c      ALPHAS(NSRC) - real    - Solubility enhancement factor due
c                               to the aqueous phase reactivity of
c                               the pollutant.
c       REACT(NSRC) - real    - Reactivity factor for each
c                               pollutant.
c                RM - real    - Mesophyll resistance (s/m)
c                               SEE NOTE #2
c       HENRY(NSRC) - real    - Henry's law constant (ratio of
c                               gas to aqueous phase concentration
c                               of the pollutant).
c              RCUT - real    - Cuticle resistance (s/m).
c
c  NOTE #1: Input units of this variable are cm**2/s.  Conversion to m**2/s
c           is made internally in the SETUP phase.
c
c  NOTE #2: Input units of s/cm are converted to s/m in the SETUP phase.

C JAT 01/29/21 ISSUE D070 TURBULENCE OPTIONS
C     Logical flags for ignoring turbulence options
C     variable is an array of size 9.  The indices are by keywrd:
C     1:  NOTURB (ignore sigma-theta and sigma-w for all hours)
C     2:  NOTURBST (ignore sigma-theta and sigma-w for stable hours only (OBULEN > 0))
C     3:  NOTURBCO (ignore sigma-theta and sigma-w for convective hours only (OBULEN < 0))
C     4:  NOSA (ignore sigma-theta for all hours)
C     5:  NOSW (ignore sigma-w for all hours)
C     6:  NOSAST (ignore sigma-theta for stable hours only (OBULEN > 0))
C     7:  NOSWST (ignore sigma-w for stable hours only (OBULEN > 0))
C     8:  NOSACO (ignore sigma-theta for convective hours only (OBULEN < 0))
C     9:  NOSWCO (ignore sigma-w for convective hours only (OBULEN < 0))
C     NOTE: ONLY THE FIRST 2 CAN BE USED WITH THE DEFAULT OPTION; THE OTHERS
C     ARE NON-DEFAULT. IF THOSE ARE ISSUED WITH THE DEFAULT KEYWORD, THE USER
C     WILL GET A WARNING MESSAGE AND THEY WILL BE IGNORED.
      LOGICAL TURBOPTS(9)
      
C**** Logical flags for met data version, L_OldMetVer is used to flag

      LOGICAL :: L_OldMetVer, L_NAD_ADJ_Flags

C**   BVF    = Brunt-Vaisala frequency
C**   BVPRIM = 0.7*BVF
C**   SFCHF  = Surface heat flux (W/sq m)
C**   USTAR  = Surface friction velocity (m/s)
C**   WSTAR  = Convective scaling velocity (m/s)
C**   VPTGZI = Vertical potential temperature gradient from ZI to ZI+500
C**            (degrees/m)
C**   ZICONV = Hourly convective mixing height estimated by AERMET (m)
C**   ZIMECH = Hourly mechanical mixing height estimated by AERMET (m)
C**   OBULEN = Monin-Obukhov length (m)
C**   SFCZ0  = Surface roughness length (m)
C**   BOWEN  = Bowen ratio = sensible heat flux/latent heat flux
C**   ALBEDO = Albedo at the earth's surface (nondimensional)
C**   UREF   = Reference height wind speed (m/s)
C**   WDREF  = Reference height wind direction (degrees from north)
C**   UREFHT = Reference height for winds (m) (first nonmissing level
C**            of wind speed AND direction above 7.0*SFCZ0)
C**   TA     = Ambient temperature at a reference height (kelvin)
C**   TREFHT = Reference height for temperature (m) (first nonmissing
C**            level of temperature)
C**   ZI     = The mixing height used by AERMOD after any manipulation
C**            and massaging (m)
C**   NPLVLS = Number of levels in the observed hourly profile data
C**   NTGLVL = Number of levels of observed potential temperature gradient
C**   IFLAG  = Top of profile flag: 1 = top level, 0 = level below top
C**   PFLHT  = Profile height above local ground level (m)
C**   PFLWD  = Profile wind direction (degrees from north)
C**   PFLWS  = Profile wind speed (m/s)
C**   PFLTA  = Profile ambient temperature (kelvins)
C**   PFLSA  = Profile sigma_A (degrees)
C**   PFLSW  = Profile sigma_W (m/s)
C**   PFLSV  = Profile sigma_V (m/s), computed from sigma_A and wind speed
C**   PFLTG  = Profile of Vertical Potential Temperature Gradient (kelvin/m)
C**   PFLTGZ = Profile of VPTG heights (midpoint of interval) (m)
C**   GRIDHT = Gridded height (m)
C**   GRIDWD = Gridded wind direction (degrees from north)
C**   GRIDWS = Gridded wind speed (m/s)
C**   RL_GRIDWS = RLine Gridded wind speed (m/s) Wind Speed Array for determining critical height Added by - WOOD 6-28-2022                                                                                                                             
C**   GRIDSW = Gridded sigma_W (m/s)
C**   GRIDSV = Gridded sigma_V (m/s)
C**   GRIDTG = Gridded vertical potential temperature gradient (deg/m)
C**   GRIDPT = Gridded potential temperature profile
C**   GRIDRHO= Gridded density profile
C**   GRIDEPS= Gridded tubulence dissipation rate (epsilon) profile for PVMRM/GRSM
C**   TG4PFL = Potential temperature gradient at 2.0 meters
C**   TG4XTR = Potential temperature gradient at 100.0 meters
C**   XLAT   = Station latitude, decimal degrees
C**   TSIGN  = Sign used for turning of wind: 1.0 for northern hemis.
C**                                          -1.0 for southern hemis.
C**   NDX4ZI = Index of gridded height immediately below ZI
C**   SVAVG  = Average sigma_V from the surface to ZI (m/s)
C**   SWAVG  = Average sigma_W from the surface to ZI (m/s)
C**   UAVG   = Average wind speed from the surface to ZI (m/s)
C**   SVATZI = sigma_V at ZI (m/s)
C**   SWATZI = sigma_W at ZI (m/s)
C**   UATZI  = Wind speed at ZI (m)
C**   PTATZI = Potential temperature at ZI (kelvin)
C**   SVATHE = Average sigma_V from the surface to HS for HS > ZI (m/s)
C**   SWATHE = Average sigma_W from the surface to HS for HS > ZI (m/s)
C**   UATHE  = Average wind speed from the surface to HS for HS > ZI (m/s)
C**   SVAVH3 = Average sigma_V from the surface to HE3 for penetrated plume
C**   SWAVH3 = Average sigma_W from the surface to HE3 for penetrated plume
C**   UAVH3  = Average wind speed from the surface to HE3 for penetrated plume
C**   SWRMAX = Residual vertical turbulence, average of measured sigma-w
C**            above ZI or 0.02 * UATZI


C***********************************************************************
C     This is The Global Variable Definition Block for Calculation
C***********************************************************************

      LOGICAL CALCS, WAKE
      LOGICAL SURFAC

      DOUBLE PRECISION PHID1, PHID2, PHIN1, PHIN2

      INTEGER :: IREC,   ISRC,   IGRP,   IAVE,   ITYP,  ISET,
     &           NUMREC, NUMSRC, NUMGRP, NUMAVE, NUMARC, NUMTYP,
     &           NUMCAP, NUMHOR,
C ---            Include counters for all source types
     &           NUMPNT, NUMVOL, NUMAREA, NUMLINE, NUMPIT,
!                Add counter for SIDEWASH Point
     &           NUMSWP,
     &           NUMFLAT, IBKGRD(6), IO3SET(6), INOXSET(6),
     &           ICYEAR, NURBSRC, NUMURB, NPD, IFVSEC,
     &           IUCAT, IOLM, NUMOLM, IPSD, NUMPSD, IURB,
     &           ITTRM,
!! Added for TTRM2
     &           CMETH,
!! End of TTRM2 insert; Nov. 2021
C**  Added for Aircraft Plume Rise; UNC-IE
     &           NAFTSRC
C**  NAFTSRC = Number of Aircraft Sources
C**  End Aircraft Plume Rise insert; April 2023

      DOUBLE PRECISION :: XS, YS, ZS, QS, HS, DS, VS, TS, SYINIT,
     &                    SZINIT, XINIT, YINIT, ANGLE, XCNTR, YCNTR,
     &                    DSFACT, DSBH, DSBW,
c --- PRIME Modification -------------------------------------------
     &                    DSBL, XADJ, YADJ, B_SUBS, B_SUBL, RSCALE,
c ------------------------------------------------------------------
     &                    D, VD, E, WDRAD, WDSIN, WDCOS, ZBASE,
C**  Added for Aircraft Plume Rise; UNC-IE
C---- AIRCRAFT Plume Rise Modification -----------------------------
     &                    MFUEL, THRUST, VAA, AFR, BYPR, SRCANGLE,
     &                    RPWR
C**  Aircraft Engine Parameters
C**    MFUEL    = Fuel Burn Rate (kg/s)
C**    THRUST   = Thrust (N)
C**    VAA      = Aircraft Velocity (m/s)
C**    AFR      = Air Fuel Ratio
C**    BYPR     = Engine By Pass Ratio
C**    SRCANGLE = Source Angle for Airborne Sources (degree)
